[Android WebView] Add AwSettings.setWebGraphicsEnabled setting

Introduce an Android WebView setting (AwSettings.setWebGraphicsEnabled /
getWebGraphicsEnabled, defaulting to true) that governs whether web
graphics APIs (WebGL 1, WebGL 2 and WebGPU) are available to content in
a WebView instance.

The setting is plumbed through JNI to
AwSettings::PopulateWebPreferencesLocked, which configures the
webgl1_enabled, webgl2_enabled and webgpu_enabled WebPreferences.

The setting is also exposed through the AndroidX WebKit support library
glue layer (WebSettingsBoundaryInterface, SupportLibWebSettingsAdapter,
SupportLibWebSettingsNoOpAdapter, Features.WEB_GRAPHICS_ENABLED and
AndroidXWebkitApiCall metrics).

Its effect is guarded by features::kWebViewWebGraphicsSwitchEnabled
(enabled by default as a kill switch). While the feature is disabled,
setWebGraphicsEnabled is a no-op and WebGL / WebGPU remain enabled.

Test: Added AwSettingsTest.testWebGraphicsEnabled* and
testWebGraphicsEnabledWebGPU* tests, which check WebGL 1 / WebGL 2
context creation and WebGPU requestAdapter() results.

[a11y-image-desc] Join Manta captions and fix annotation scoring

Unlike the legacy server (which pre-joined caption parts on the server),
Manta returns PRIMARY_CAPTION, SECONDARY_CAPTION (with uncalibrated
scores > 1.0), and LABEL as separate entries. Picking a single non-OCR
entry by highest raw score dropped secondary captions and allowed a
higher-scoring single-word LABEL to overwrite a full-sentence caption.

Update Annotator::OnMantaResponseReceived to:
- Track PRIMARY_CAPTION, SECONDARY_CAPTION, LABEL, and OCR separately
  instead of comparing raw scores across different annotation types.
- Join PRIMARY_CAPTION and SECONDARY_CAPTION with a newline and average
  their clamped (<= 1.0) scores, ignoring orphan secondary captions.
- Fall back to LABEL only when no primary caption is present, and record
  UMA metrics once per emitted annotation.

[GCM] Use USER_VISIBLE blocking task priority behind a feature flag

Profile GCM services create their blocking task runner with
TaskPriority::BEST_EFFORT, causing BestEffortTaskInhibitingPolicy
execution fences to delay GCMKeyStore and GCMStoreImpl loads for up to
minutes while Web Push, Sharing, and Sync invalidations await storage
callbacks.

Introduce gcm::features::kGCMBlockingTaskRunnerUserVisible (disabled by
default) to switch the profile GCM blocking task runner to
TaskPriority::USER_VISIBLE across Desktop, Android, and iOS, and enable
it in fieldtrial_testing_config.json.

net/socket: Record used DNS answer and Optimistic DNS UMAs for TCP/SSL

Instrument TcpConnectJob and SSLConnectJob to record the age, cache
freshness status, and wire TTL expiration of the DNS answer ultimately
used to establish TCP and SSL/TLS connections, as well as Optimistic
DNS diagnostic histograms:

- Record Net.DNS.UsedAnswer.Tcp.* and
  Net.DNS.OptimisticDns.Tcp.StaleIpMatchFreshDns.* in TcpConnectJob.
- Propagate used_answer_freshness_status and cache_staleness from
  TcpConnectJob to SSLConnectJob and record Net.DNS.UsedAnswer.Ssl.*
  and Net.DNS.OptimisticDns.SSLConnect.*.

WebTransport: Handle BYOB datagram edge cases

Align incoming datagram reads with WebTransport's BYOB semantics. Empty
datagrams remain buffered until the reader choice is known, are skipped
while an active BYOB read is pending, and are delivered to default
readers as an empty Uint8Array, matching WebTransport PR #800.

Track the original BYOB view type in pull-into descriptors and validate
the active replacement request after releaseLock(). Preserve the
specified RangeError-before-TypeError ordering and safely handle
zero-length internal byte-stream queue entries.

This supports the BYOB tests proposed in:
https://github.com/web-platform-tests/wpt/pull/64026

Mozilla bug: https://bugzilla.mozilla.org/show_bug.cgi?id=2007755

[extensions] Reduce heap allocations in tabs and windows query paths.

`extensions::ServiceWorkerHost::RequestWorker` shows elevated browser
UI-thread congestion under PartitionAlloc `SchedulerLoopQuarantine`,
with 99.4% of `ExtensionFunction::RunWithValidation` samples coming from
`TabsQueryFunction::Run` and `WindowsGetAllFunction::Run` via
`ExtensionTabUtil::CreateTabObject`. Rather than excluding
`RequestWorker` from safety checks (which would disable quarantine
across the extension API surface), reduce transient heap allocations
along these hot paths:

- Store `ExtensionFunction::response_type_` inline as
  `std::optional<ResponseType>` rather than `unique_ptr<ResponseType>`.
- Gate populating `url`, `pending_url`, `title`, and `fav_icon_url` in
  `ExtensionTabUtil::CreateTabObject` on `ScrubTabBehavior` instead of
  allocating and immediately resetting up to 4 strings on scrubbed tabs.
- Precompute the UTF-16 `title_pattern` once before iterating tabs in
  `TabsQueryFunction::BuildTabList`, return `ScrubTabBehavior` directly
  from `MatchesTab` on match (computing `GetScrubTabBehavior` once), and
  forward both `ScrubTabBehavior` and `TabListInterface` to
  `CreateTabObject`.
- Reserve `tab_list` capacity in
  `BrowserExtensionWindowController::CreateTabList`.
- Replace `TabListInterface::GetAllTabs()` (`std::vector` allocation +
  linear scan) in `ExtensionTabUtil::GetTabListInterface` with
  `TabListInterface::GetIndexOfTab(tab_interface->GetHandle())`,
  resolving crbug.com/415961057 for callers where `tab_list` is null.

Pinpoint (`desktop_ui` custom story with 50 open tabs, MV3 service
worker running 400 iterations of `tabs.query` + `windows.getAll` with
browser UI-thread `SchedulerLoopQuarantine` enabled):
- win-11-perf:
  https://pinpoint-dot-chromeperf.appspot.com/job/1276e38b990000
  - `TabsQueryFunction:Run`: 0.170 ms -> 0.156 ms (-8.1%, p < 0.001)
  - `ServiceWorkerHost:RequestWorker`:
    0.302 ms -> 0.289 ms (-4.3%, p < 0.001)
  - `BrowserExtensionWindowController:ToValue`:
    0.263 ms -> 0.251 ms (-4.3%, p < 0.001)
  - `tabs_windows_query_cuj`: 631.76 ms -> 613.68 ms (-2.9%, p < 0.001)
- linux-r350-perf:
  https://pinpoint-dot-chromeperf.appspot.com/job/15b348ad990000
  - `TabsQueryFunction:Run`: 0.142 ms -> 0.128 ms (-9.8%, p < 0.001)
  - `ServiceWorkerHost:RequestWorker`:
    0.276 ms -> 0.262 ms (-5.0%, p < 0.001)
  - `BrowserExtensionWindowController:ToValue`:
    0.217 ms -> 0.206 ms (-5.0%, p < 0.001)
  - `tabs_windows_query_cuj`: 596.49 ms -> 581.75 ms (-2.5%, p = 0.001)

58528f1702afe1d233ded2f0e14ee1f814ec0944 [ diff ] 
Don't give generic MIME handlers the PDF viewer background

When a third-party extension handles application/pdf through the
generic MIME handler API, the page Chrome wraps around its viewer gets
the built-in PDF viewer's dark background, rgb(40,40,40). It shows
through any transparent area of the viewer.

The wrapper looks the color up in the plugin info registered for the
MIME type. Only plugin handlers register plugin info, so for a generic
handler the lookup finds the built-in PDF viewer's entry. This
regressed in crrev.com/c/8379203, which started applying that color to
the OOPIF wrapper.

Fix by taking the color of the OOPIF-based page from the handling
extension's MimeTypesHandler.

e504bb0a629a53caa1abbbc0bf4e6f3dbeb0d0d0 [ diff ] 
[Blink] Add webgpu_enabled WebPreference and Setting

Blink has per-page WebPreferences for WebGL 1 and WebGL 2
(webgl1_enabled and webgl2_enabled), but had no equivalent per-page
preference to disable WebGPU for a specific WebView / Page.

Add a per-page webgpu_enabled preference (defaulting to true):
- Add webgpu_enabled to blink::web_pref::WebPreferences, its mojom and
  StructTraits, WebSettings/WebSettingsImpl and the webGPUEnabled
  Setting in settings.json5.
- Propagate webGPUEnabled into WorkerSettings so that dedicated workers
  inherit the value from their parent document.
- In GPU::requestAdapter(), when disabled by settings, log a console
  warning ("WebGPU has been disabled by the embedder.") and resolve the
  returned promise with null (matching the WebGPU spec requirement that
  requestAdapter() resolves to null rather than rejecting when an
  adapter cannot be provided).

This preference will be used by Android WebView's follow-up
AwSettings.setWebGraphicsEnabled setting.

Remove unused auto_spanification_helper.h includes

These files include base/containers/auto_spanification_helper.h but
don't use anything it defines. Remove the include, so the header can be
deleted once its remaining users are migrated.

The helper also includes <array>, base/containers/span.h and
base/numerics/checked_math.h. The files here that use them already get
them from their other includes.

3a3face5932531ab74b8ba4656e3a49838e00901

4003007fcb72c3c785b9032c3cc203d13a331424 [ diff ] 
[3p freshness] opus: Roll to v1.6.1 and enable auto-update

Roll from 55513e81 (84 commits after v1.5.2) to v1.6.1 (22244de5),
the newest release, and let Crowbar roll opus from now on.

- crowbar.txtpb: latest {} follows the release tags vX.Y and vX.Y.Z
  (v1.6, v1.6.1). It uses generic {} with digits only, not semver {}:
  semver {} also picks pre-release tags (opus has v1.3-rc, v1.2-beta),
  and Crowbar has no tag filter (b/552777259). The regex follows
  PDFium's third_party/lcms/crowbar.txtpb. The upstream is v1.6.1,
  written by crowbar resolve and crowbar promote. not_valid_until is
  deleted: the bake-in delay is for CI rolls, this CL rolls now.
- patches/0003-nsq-ignore-wraparound.patch is deleted: v1.6 has
  upstream 1c370855, and the patch no longer applies:
    error: patch failed: src/silk/NSQ.c:348
- patches/0004-avx2-rounding-shift-overflow.patch is regenerated on
  v1.6.1 (same +/- lines, new context): v1.6.1 does not have upstream
  212d66c2 yet, and the old patch fails with
    error: patch failed: src/silk/x86/NSQ_del_dec_avx2.c:97
  0001 applies unchanged.
- patches/0002-plain-subtraction-outside-fuzzing.patch is deleted, so
  silk/NSQ_del_dec.c and silk/x86/NSQ_del_dec_sse4_1.c are now the
  v1.6.1 files. The patch kept plain subtraction outside fuzzing
  builds and silk_SUB32_ovflw() only in fuzzing builds. Upstream
  cfeddc49 (2023) uses silk_SUB32_ovflw() in all builds: it gives the
  same result as the plain subtraction when there is no overflow, and
  defined wraparound instead of undefined behavior when there is.
- copy.bara.sky: v1.6.1 deletes celt/mips/vq_mipsr1.h and
  silk/mips/NSQ_del_dec_mipsr1.h and adds two headers that built files
  include: celt/arm/mathops_arm.h (BUILD.gn defines
  OPUS_ARM_MAY_HAVE_NEON_INTR; without it the arm64 build fails with
  "fatal error: 'arm/mathops_arm.h' file not found") and
  silk/mips/NSQ_mips.h, which replaces NSQ_del_dec_mipsr1.h for __mips.
- Public API: no function signatures change. opus_defines.h renames
  the __opus_check_*() helper macros to opus_check_*() (no Chromium
  code uses them) and adds the QEXT, OSCE_BWE and IGNORE_EXTENSIONS
  CTLs and OPUS_APPLICATION_RESTRICTED_{SILK,CELT}, which Chromium
  does not call. The new QEXT, DRED and OSCE code is behind
  ENABLE_QEXT/ENABLE_DRED/ENABLE_OSCE, which BUILD.gn does not set.
  The library sources BUILD.gn builds are the same.
- DEPS: allow "xtensa/..." includes, like arm/, mips/ and x86/.
  silk/SigProc_FIX.h and silk/macros.h include xtensa/ headers behind
  OPUS_XTENSA_LX7, which BUILD.gn does not define; checkdeps fails
  without the rule:
    Illegal include: "xtensa/SigProc_FIX_lx7.h"
- README.chromium: Version and Revision from crowbar promote; the
  patch list drops 0002 and 0003.
- src/: crowbar output.

Made with:
    $ crowbar resolve --no-dry-run ./third_party/opus
    $ crowbar promote --no-dry-run ./third_party/opus
    $ crowbar build --no-dry-run ./third_party/opus

031407500ca8bdd5d6d9076d2623ff137ae94226

3ad974b4ea4aad1520f843e02acdfac61d2fcdc1 [ diff ] 
Inject AppService into AddSupervisionHandler

AddSupervisionHandler only uses AppRegistryCache of AppServiceProxy,
which was looked up from the WebUI's Profile via
AppServiceProxyFactory.

Because AddSupervisionHandler is tied to the specific user session,
this CL changes it to take apps::AppService in its constructor.
AddSupervisionUI resolves it from apps::AppServiceRegistry with the
AccountId of the Profile, as same as it does for IdentityManager and
SupervisedUserService.

With this CL, //chrome/browser/ui/webui/ash/add_supervision no longer
depends on //chrome/browser/apps/app_service.

Bug: 477191550

tree
57b9ba734f7d0691b9a1b26871867b8f4d0c6ed6
parent
00f466fe8b16f71494c55a9e79057987808b5d7a [ diff ] 
Fix caching for built-in strategy size functions

The Streams standard associates cached strategy size functions with the
realm's `globalObject` (the inner `Window`), whereas
`GetCachedSizeFunction` was caching them on `v8::Context::Global()`,
which in V8 returns the `WindowProxy`. Cache them in `V8PerContextData`
instead. This matches the semantics required by the standard, except for
an intentional minor deviation when contexts are detached (to avoid
retaining V8 objects from detached realms), and matches how other
per-context caches work in Blink.

tree
0a591dd3ec510f5d49df23679c7c0b54d25299c3
parent
ef03a2b1896dcd423f6465d4361e8ce3bb13281a [ diff ] 
updater: give PolicyStatus noexcept move operations

PolicyStatus<T> declared its copy constructor and copy assignment as
`= default`. Any user-declared copy operation suppresses the implicit
move operations, so every move of a PolicyStatus (for example, getter
return values that are not elided, or values bound into callbacks) deep
copied its vector of entries and its two optional entries.

Declare defaulted noexcept move operations. Also move the by-value
policy argument into PolicyStatus::Entry instead of copying it, and
include <type_traits> and <utility> for std::is_same_v and std::move.

Bug: None

[3p freshness] opus: Make patches/ reproduce src/

src/ differs from upstream 55513e81 in five files, and README.chromium
only lists the changes in prose. Record each change as a patch, so
applying patches/ to upstream 55513e81 gives the checked-in src/, and
the Crowbar workflow (https://crrev.com/c/8781194) can apply them.
src/ does not change.

- 0001-no-assertion-messages.patch: CHROMIUM_NO_LOGGING in
  celt/arch.h.
- 0002-plain-subtraction-outside-fuzzing.patch: the fuzzing-only
  silk_SUB32_ovflw() blocks in silk/NSQ_del_dec.c and
  silk/x86/NSQ_del_dec_sse4_1.c. Upstream cfeddc49 (2023) then used
  silk_SUB32_ovflw() for these subtractions in all builds; the 2025
  roll kept Chromium's #else branches, so the patch now keeps the plain
  subtraction outside fuzzing builds.
- 0003-nsq-ignore-wraparound.patch: the backport of upstream 1c370855
  in silk/NSQ.c.
- 0004-avx2-rounding-shift-overflow.patch: the AVX2 rounding fix in
  silk/x86/NSQ_del_dec_avx2.c; upstream took the same lines as
  212d66c2.
- README.chromium: Local Modifications lists the patches and replaces
  the "Local changes" prose; the apply command runs from this
  directory.

Each patch touches its own files. Applying them to the upstream
55513e81 copies of the 271 src/ files gives the checked-in src/:

    $ git clone -q https://gitlab.xiph.org/xiph/opus /tmp/opus
    $ mkdir -p /tmp/o/src && cp -r third_party/opus/patches /tmp/o
    $ git -C /tmp/opus archive 55513e81 \
        $(git -C third_party/opus/src ls-files) | tar -x -C /tmp/o/src
    $ (cd /tmp/o && for p in patches/*; do patch -s -p1 < $p; done)
    $ diff -r /tmp/o/src third_party/opus/src    # no output

[LifetimeSafety] Add LIFETIME_BOUND to prefs & autofill

Mechanically adds LIFETIME_BOUND inferred by Clang
-Wlifetime-safety-suggestions and tools/clang/lifetime_safety/
add_annotations.py (v2) across //components/prefs (8 headers, including
ScopedDictPrefUpdate::Get() and PrefService) and
//components/autofill/core/browser/suggestions (2 headers, including
CreditCardSuggestionGenerator).

These compile-time annotations provide hints for Clang to warn if
annotated function parameters or return values. are used outside
their apparent lifetimes. For a primer, see:
https://docs.google.com/document/d/1Ro1RMVNsVDqeZpeiY-NR8sPXTI8yy3Ov5cHM7I-DMEo/edit?tab=t.0

These annotations unlocked two cross-TU production use-after-free bugs
during the whole-codebase fixpoint run:
1. chrome/browser/ui/signin/promos/signin_promo_util.cc:162 (via
   ScopedDictPrefUpdate(prefs, ...)->FindDict(...))
2. chrome/browser/actor/actor_form_filling_service_impl.cc:378 (via
   CreditCardSuggestionGenerator::four_digit_combinations_in_dom_)

[BrowserActuator] Deliver actuation prompt via One Platform stream

Previously, BrowserActuatorMessageHandler accepted all feature payloads
(including TriggerActuationRequest carrying the actuation prompt)
bundled inside the FCM SharingMessage alongside StartSession.

Now that the server sends the actuation prompt over the One Platform
WatchSessions connection instead of bundling it into the FCM message,
update BrowserActuatorMessageHandler::OnMessage so FCM only processes
StartSession and DeviceOptInRequest payloads, while actuation prompts
are received over the One Platform stream.

f77a953c1061984a98fda3cfdff0f29e6350f4e1
parent
6d970e66052cfd15a13c938336b1651c3908f22f [ diff ] 
Fix flake in frameworks-dom-xhr-event-breakpoints-1.js

Fix is Await UI.Widget.Widget.allUpdatesComplete at the start of
testXHRBreakpoint() so pending widget updates and status notifications
from resuming testDOMBreakpoint() finish before registering the next
pause sniffer.

When testDOMBreakpoint() resumes execution via
SourcesPanel.togglePause(), #clearInterface() invokes
#updateDebuggerButtonsAndStatus() while
debuggerModel.debuggerPausedDetails() still holds the previous DOM
breakpoint details. If Debugger.resumed arrives before the next
animation frame, #debuggerResumed() clears the DebuggerPausedMessage
view input to null and testXHRBreakpoint() starts and registers a
one-shot sniffer on updateDebuggerButtonsAndStatusForTest() via
waitUntilPausedAndDumpStackAndResume(). When the animation frame fires
before the XHR breakpoint pauses, the in-flight
#updateDebuggerButtonsAndStatus() call from togglePause() resolves
updateComplete and invokes updateDebuggerButtonsAndStatusForTest() with
an empty paused status message, consuming testXHRBreakpoint()'s sniffer
prematurely.

Test history:
https://ci.chromium.org/ui/test/chromium/%3A%2F%2F%5C%3Ablink_web_tests%21webtest%3A%3Ahttp%2Ftests%2Fdevtools%2Fsources%2Fdebugger-frameworks%23frameworks-dom-xhr-event-breakpoints-1.js

Revert CHECK_NE in AXPosition::CreateTextPosition to DCHECK_NE

A recent bulk check conversion change converted a DCHECK_NE in
AXPosition::CreateTextPosition to a CHECK_NE. This caused crashes when
anchor.tree()->GetAXTreeID() returned AXTreeIDUnknown().

This change reverts the CHECK_NE back to DCHECK_NE in
AXPosition::CreateTextPosition and adds a TODO comment to convert it
back to a CHECK once we are confident it will not be triggered.

This fix was generated by the Crash Autofix pipeline for crash report
crash/e2b1d4ec08400000. Please see go/crash-autofix for more details.

Revert CHECK_EQ to DCHECK_EQ in WebAppIconManager::EncodeAndWriteIcon

Non-square icon bitmaps can be encountered when encoding and writing
icons in web applications. The recent conversion of DCHECK_EQ to
CHECK_EQ caused crashes in release builds when processing non-square
bitmaps. This change reverts CHECK_EQ back to DCHECK_EQ in
EncodeAndWriteIcon to prevent crashes in production.

This fix was generated by the Crash Autofix pipeline for crash report
crash/4e714b08af800000. Please see go/crash-autofix for more details.

[3p freshness] opus: Remove files Chromium does not build

The Crowbar workflow (https://crrev.com/c/8781194) imports only the
src/ files that BUILD.gn builds, the headers they include, and the
license files COPYING and LICENSE_PLEASE_READ.txt. Remove the other
436 files from src/ first, so the workflow leaves src/ unchanged:

- dnn/ (301 files), the neural network PLC, DRED and OSCE code, and
  celt/x86/x86_arch_macros.h, which only dnn/ includes.
- silk/fixed/*.c and its arm, mips and x86 variants: the fixed-point
  encoder. BUILD.gn builds the float one and never defines FIXED_POINT.
- celt/arm/ run-time CPU detection, NE10 and assembly files, and
  silk/arm/arm_silk_map.c; BUILD.gn presumes the ARM features instead.
- Upstream tests, demos and tools that BUILD.gn does not build:
  celt/tests/, silk/tests/, celt/dump_modes/, celt/opus_custom_demo.c,
  src/repacketizer_demo.c, tests/test_opus_{custom,dred,extensions,
  projection}.c, tests/opus_decode_fuzzer.* and tests/*.sh.
- Build files and docs: the autotools, CMake and Meson files, the
  hand-written Makefile.mips and Makefile.unix, the pkg-config
  templates, opus.m4, cmake/, m4/, meson/, doc/, scripts/, training/,
  and the root AUTHORS, ChangeLog, NEWS, README, README.draft,
  releases.sha2, tar_list.txt and update_version.

None of the deleted files is in a GN target:

    $ gn refs out/Default $(git show --diff-filter=D --name-only \
        --format= HEAD | sed 's#^#//#')
    The input matches no targets, configs, or files.

Built files still include dnn/ headers, config.h, custom_support.h and
Blackfin headers, but only behind macros BUILD.gn does not define
(ENABLE_DEEP_PLC, ENABLE_DRED, ENABLE_OSCE, ENABLE_LOSSGEN,
HAVE_CONFIG_H, CUSTOM_SUPPORT, BFIN_ASM).

Also:
- convert_rtcd_assembler.py: unused since 22c7a36c71506; its inputs
  arm2gnu.pl and celt_pitch_xcorr_arm.s are deleted here.
- README.chromium drops the "Local changes" items for .gitignore and
  the executable bits of autogen.bat and the dnn/ scripts: those files
  are gone.
- infra/inclusive_language_presubmit_exempt_dirs.txt drops
  third_party/opus/src/doc, which no longer exists.

parent
2c1cdf4e99a1f8e5eb8f0a61479f3a13dfbabdcc [ diff ] 
autocorrect: Delay `ScopedDictPrefUpdate` destruction

This CL lines up a shot to add `LIFETIME_BOUND` annotations to prefs and
autofill. The Chrome CPP Runtime Safety team would like to impose a
precondition that `Value`s gotten from a `ScopedDictPrefUpdate` must not
outlive the same (since the notification fires in
`~ScopedDictPrefUpdate()`).

Note that there is no actual correctness issue here: the lifetime of the
`Value` (owned by the pref store) is fine, and the code here only cares
that its address was not `nullptr`.

[Public Transit] Migrate loadUrlOnUiThread() callers that wait

Callers that started a load with ChromeTabUtils#loadUrlOnUiThread()
and then waited with waitForTabPageLoaded() now load the page in a
Trip, so a failed load throws TravelException with the state of each
Condition instead of a bare timeout:
- DocumentPictureInPictureActivityTest and BottomSheetControllerTest
  load from their page Station (loadWebPageProgrammatically(),
  loadAboutBlank()).
- TwaInstallerIntegrationTest and WebApkIntegrationTest use
  WebPageStation#waitForWebPageLoad() since they have no Station.

DocumentPictureInPictureActivityTest is @DisabledTest, so its change is
only compile-checked.

TabPersistentStoreIntegrationTest and RuntimePermissionTestUtils are
left for follow-ups. loadUrlOnUiThread() stays for callers that don't
wait for the load.

[ServiceWorker] Customize aborted fetch() error for race requests
📋 Metadata
commit
14ffc990aa4f27b2e4f2b2c4c9c1c9567d389c89
[ log ]
author

Wed Nov 11 05:42:23 2026
committer
Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Wed Nov 11 05:42:23 2026
tree
4b6373abf402efacdde24b28d93fcec3e636d00b
parent
6a9363db152f78a0db74cee4a0e00a82686ab903 [ diff ] 
[ServiceWorker] Customize aborted fetch() error for race requests

When a ServiceWorker static router rule uses the
race-network-and-fetch-handler source and the raced network request
wins or the navigation is cancelled before the fetch handler's
fetch(event.request) receives response headers, the underlying loader
completes with net::ERR_ABORTED. Previously, fetch() rejected with a
generic TypeError ("Failed to fetch"), making benign race cancellations
indistinguishable from genuine network failures in ServiceWorker error
telemetry. By contrast, NavigationPreloadRequest::OnComplete() uses a
distinct cancellation message when preloadResponse fails with
net::ERR_ABORTED before settling.

Introduce kServiceWorkerRaceNetworkRequestAbortedErrorMessage
(disabled by default). When enabled, if a ServiceWorker fetch() backed
by the race-network-and-fetch-handler loader (kRaceLoaderUsed) fails
with net::ERR_ABORTED,
ServiceWorkerGlobalScope::GetCustomFetchErrorMessage() returns:
"The service worker race-network-and-fetch-handler request was
cancelled before 'fetch()' settled."
FetchManager::Loader::DidFail() then overrides the rejection message
via ResponseResolver::OverrideTypeErrorMessage().

While the Fetch specification requires rejecting with a TypeError, the
error message text itself ("Failed to fetch") is not defined by the
standard. Because this error is only surfaced to the ServiceWorker of
the site that configured the race-network-and-fetch-handler rule, the
message only indicates that the site's own ServiceWorker routing was
used and does not expose any cross-origin information.

Subsequent fetch(event.request) calls that fall back to the default
loader (kFallbackDueToAlreadyConsumed), non-ERR_ABORTED network errors,
AbortController aborts, and post-header stream cancellations preserve
existing behavior.

Covered by unit tests in service_worker_global_scope_test.cc and
parameterized browser tests in service_worker_browsertest.cc across
flag-off and flag-on states.

7b2b19acd60c7f1d850f0c761242bbf03c40b342 [ diff ] 
[Public Transit] Add page Station helpers for waitForTabPageLoaded()

Tests without a Station each copied the same hop-on block to replace
ChromeTabUtils#waitForTabPageLoaded(). Add a shared API for its two
forms, as asked on crrev.com/c/8741305:

- BasePageStation#waitForPage(tab, url, builder): hops on at the page
  |tab| shows once |url| is loaded.
- BasePageStation#waitForPageLoad(trip, tab, url, builder): also waits
  for the load the trigger starts to stop, so it covers reloads,
  same-URL and back/forward cache navigations.
- WebPageStation#waitForWebPage(tab, url) and
  WebPageStation#waitForWebPageLoad(trip, tab, url): shortcuts for web
  pages.
- Builder#initForLoadingUrlOnTab(url, tab): builds the Station from a
  Tab when there is no previous Station.
- BasePageStation#runOnUiThreadToNewPageLoad(): the same wait for tests
  that hold a Station.

Differences from waitForTabPageLoaded(): |url| is matched as a
substring, |tab| must be its Activity's current tab, and the Station
also waits for the page to be interactable with hit-test data.

PageLoadStoppedCondition moves out of ChromeTabUtils so both waits use
it. Point the waitForTabPageLoaded() @deprecated note at the new
helpers, and migrate WarmupManagerTest and WebAppHeaderLayoutBrowserTest.

[webapps] Scope TaskEnvironment to Manifest dispatch-context tests

crrev.com/c/8527601 added a base::test::TaskEnvironment to the shared
ManifestMojomTraitsTest fixture, so every traits test spun up a full
thread pool even though only the two tests that construct a
mojo::internal::MessageDispatchContext need a sequence (the context
lives in sequence-local storage).

On mac-ubsan-fyi-rel, UBSan's vptr check emits reports that appear to be
false positives across the whole suite: in the failing run (~18.5k
reports), every report we inspected names a dynamic type that is the
checked type itself or a subclass of it (e.g. a base::SampleVector
checked as base::HistogramSamples), in base/, gtest, url/ and libc++
code. Each code location reports once per process, so the first test in
a batch to start a thread pool prints ~4MB of reports.
ManifestMojomTraitsTest.ImageResourceEmptySrcFails was that test, so the
launcher marked it EXCESSIVE_OUTPUT (limit 500KB) even though the test
itself passed, and LUCI Bisection blamed the CL. This CL only removes
the unneeded thread pool from these tests; the vptr reports themselves
are a builder-wide issue that this CL does not address.

Move the two dispatch-context tests to a new
ManifestMojomTraitsDispatchContextTest fixture that uses the lighter
SingleThreadTaskEnvironment (no thread pool), and leave the other traits
tests with no task environment. Also switch those two tests to
mojo::test::SerializeAndDeserialize.

Revert CHECK_GE to DCHECK_GE for window tree host offset y

In release builds, offset.y() can evaluate to a value less than zero in
DesktopWindowTreeHostWin::GetBoundsInAcceleratedWidgetPixelCoordinates,
causing a crash via CHECK_GE.

Revert CHECK_GE back to DCHECK_GE for offset.y() to prevent crashes in
release builds while maintaining assertions in debug builds.

This fix was generated by the Crash Autofix pipeline for crash report
crash/91c4472c08400000. Please see go/crash-autofix for more details.

gpu: Fix ExternalVk release layout for images without color attachment

ExternalVkImageBacking::Create() only adds
VK_IMAGE_USAGE_COLOR_ATTACHMENT_BIT for write usages, so read-only
images lack it, e.g. GLES2_READ images when
GL_ANGLE_memory_object_flags is supported (since 42425e09bfbb) and
WEBGPU_READ-only images. However, BeginAccess() and UploadToVkImage()
release VkImages to the external queue family for GL and Dawn access in
VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_COLOR_ATTACHMENT_OPTIMAL, and GetVkImageLayoutsForGL()
reports GL_LAYOUT_COLOR_ATTACHMENT_EXT to glWaitSemaphoreEXT() and
glSignalSemaphoreEXT(). Skia and ANGLE then record barriers to or from
that layout on images without color attachment usage, violating
VUID-VkImageMemoryBarrier-oldLayout-01208.

Add GetLayoutForExternalAccess(), which returns
VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_SHADER_READ_ONLY_OPTIMAL for such images
(VK_IMAGE_USAGE_SAMPLED_BIT is always set), and use it in BeginAccess()
and UploadToVkImage(). The USE_WEBGPU_ON_VULKAN_VIA_GL_INTEROP path of
BeginAccess() transitioned images to
VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_TRANSFER_DST_OPTIMAL but recorded
VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_COLOR_ATTACHMENT_OPTIMAL; it now transitions to and
records the layout from GetLayoutForExternalAccess(). Also only chain
VkProtectedSubmitInfo in VulkanCommandBuffer::Submit() when
allow_protected_memory is true (omitting it is equivalent to VK_FALSE
per the Vulkan spec, and SwiftShader aborts when it is chained).

Dawn now also receives VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_SHADER_READ_ONLY_OPTIMAL for
images without color attachment usage, so
ExternalVkImageDawnImageRepresentation no longer adds RenderAttachment
internal usage for them.

Add unit tests covering all three release paths (Ganesh Vulkan
BeginAccess(), WebGPU-via-GL-interop BeginAccess(), and
UploadToVkImage() for both GL and Dawn). Filter the new tests on the
Linux TSan SwiftShader bot (crbug.com/462477487, crbug.com/462477489).

haptics: Add Android backend in services/device/haptics

Implements the Android hardware backend (HapticsManagerAndroid) for
device.mojom.HapticsService. See go/web-haptics-design for more
information.

- Uses VibrationEffect.Composition on Android 11+ (API 30+) when the
  system vibrator supports the required primitives, scaling amplitude by
  the normalized intensity parameter in [0.0, 1.0] (with a 0.7f
  full-strength scale aligned with AOSP and AGSA).
- Falls back from API 31 primitives (PRIMITIVE_LOW_TICK for tick,
  PRIMITIVE_THUD for edge) to intensity-scaled API 30 primitives
  (PRIMITIVE_TICK at 0.6f and PRIMITIVE_CLICK at 0.7f), and silently
  no-ops on Android 10 (API 29) or hardware lacking primitive support
  rather than playing unscaled predefined waveforms.
- Tags vibrations with VibrationAttributes.USAGE_TOUCH (API 33+) or
  AudioAttributes.USAGE_ASSISTANCE_SONIFICATION (API 30-32) so OS touch
  feedback settings and Do Not Disturb are respected.
- Adds Robolectric JUnit coverage (HapticsManagerAndroidTest) and
  updates services/device/haptics/README.md.

11da3e1af8b4bb0eec7648b7f4f734d32142c10e [ diff ] 
[PageLoadMetrics] Fix image UMA tests on high-DPI displays

ReportImagePixelInaccuracy scales CSS layout dimensions by the
document's device pixel ratio. On a 2x Retina/high-DPI display, the
image UMA tests record 120*120 - (50*2)^2 = 4400 overfetched pixels
instead of the expected 11900 at 1x.

Pin the base fixture to a 1x device scale factor so histogram
expectations are independent of the host display. Pass the scale through
the fixture constructor and set the switch once, preserving the high-DPI
fixture's explicit 2.1x scale.

Re-enable PictureWithIncorrectSizesAttribute on Mac, preserving the
existing Windows disable for ImageWithCorrectSizesAttribute. The five
scale-sensitive tests reproduce the mismatch without the fix on Mac, and
all seven image UMA tests pass with it.

test_fonts: Replace fonts.conf TODO with an explanatory comment

The render-setting aliases in the test fonts.conf (NonAntiAliasedSans,
SlightHintedGeorgia, SubpixelPositioning, ...) carried a TODO to make
them test-specific. They are used by font_loader_unittest and by the
virtual/text-antialias/* web tests, which run under the shared test
fontconfig; content_shell has no per-test fontconfig configuration, so
making them test-specific would need new plumbing for little benefit.

Replace the TODO with a comment that explains why the aliases live
here and which tests depend on them. Comment-only change.

[bisect-builds] List only milestones in range for release builds

For release builds (-r), bisect-builds.py lists the whole release
bucket to find versions, e.g. ~20k versions in
gs://chrome-unsigned/android-B0urB0N, which takes ~6 minutes.

When the good and bad versions are known, list only the milestones in
range, in parallel, with `gsutil ls -d <bucket>/<milestone>.*`. This
takes ~3 minutes for M150-M156.

102f0ec2c9dce0d536d1a80a411b1b20b6838711
parent
4f5b4eff7733b206780d0817b81456551df040a2 [ diff ] 
[Media] Fix MultiBuffer origin failure, retries, and UrlIndex eviction

1. Only mark UrlData as CORS cross-origin in UrlData::CrossOriginFail()
   when no response has established a data origin yet (!data_origin_),
   so subsequent blocked requests cannot mutate the taint state of an
   already-established UrlData. Abort orphaned in-flight providers once
   UrlData::HasFailed() is true so they cannot mutate or hijack shared
   UrlIndex entries.

2. Track per-request received payload bytes (received_data_) before
   retrying in DidFinishLoading() so 0-byte responses (even when earlier
   blocks are already cached in MultiBuffer) and short opaque or
   non-range Service Worker responses fail immediately rather than
   retrying.

3. Mark UrlData as failed in UrlData::Fail() so UrlData::Valid() and
   UrlData::MergeFrom() reject failed entries, and immediately evict
   0-block failed entries via OnEmpty().

4. Track opaque Service Worker provenance explicitly via
   UrlData::HasOpaqueServiceWorkerResponse() so opaque Service Worker
   responses require exact URL matching (even if
   kDisallowMixedOpaqueMediaResponses is disabled), cannot mix with
   direct network opaque responses across range requests on the same
   UrlData under kDisallowMixedOpaqueMediaResponses, use unindexed
   UrlData on redirects rather than colliding with UrlIndex, skip
   UrlIndex::TryInsert() and UrlData::MergeFrom(), and reject
   mid-resource 200 OK opaque Service Worker responses.

5. Validate response status, range bounds, length compatibility, and
   flags propagated from old_url_data in RedirectTo() before mutating
   metadata on or migrating to destination_url_data, so failed responses
   do not clobber or invalidate shared UrlIndex entries.

6. Propagate pinned resource lengths across UrlData::RedirectTo() and
   HTTP 416 responses, reject mid-resource HTTP 416 responses when
   byte_pos() < length(), and verify length compatibility for non-HTTP
   responses.

Fixed: 576132668, 576134575, 576134162, 581380020

43656628ee647b3f8a5508ac21b3124ec2c567e2 [ diff ] 
[bisect-builds] Check release archives in parallel

For release builds (-r), bisect-builds.py checks which versions have the
archive by running `gsutil ls` on batches of 100 paths sequentially.
gsutil checks each path sequentially at ~1s per path, so a range with
~700 versions took ~12 minutes.

Run smaller batches of 25 paths in parallel with up to 16 gsutil
processes, which takes ~70s for the same range.

Also deduplicate the result: depot_tools/gsutil.py re-runs a failed
command (e.g. when some paths don't exist), so a path can be listed
twice.

Bug: None

8aea8e5e89a773514dd542e043b4fa6fda6b0cb9
parent
562dd8fdee5a1354757f38c6a87b46caec274d28 [ diff ] 
[WebGL] Dispose DrawingBuffer in pre-finalizer

DrawingBuffer is off-heap and ref-counted by cc::TextureLayer while
holding an untraced client_ pointer to the Oilpan-managed
WebGLRenderingContextBase. When a WebGL canvas leaves the DOM in a frame
that ends before paint, cc::LayerTreeHost retains the cc::TextureLayer
after the context becomes unreachable. Previously,
WebGLRenderingContextBase::Dispose() deferred DrawingBuffer teardown
until ~WebGLRenderingContextBase() during incremental sweeping, allowing
cc::TextureLayer::Update() to call
DrawingBuffer::PrepareTransferableResource() and dereference client_
after Oilpan poisoned the dead context.

Perform context teardown, DrawingBuffer::BeginDestruction(), and evicted
context restoration in WebGLRenderingContextBase::Dispose() during
Oilpan pre-finalization so DrawingBuffer clears client_ and detaches its
cc::TextureLayer via ClearClient() before sweeping begins. Because pre-
finalizers run under DisallowGarbageCollectionScope (including during
worker thread termination after thread-local Persistents are cleared),
look up the active and evicted context sets via TryActiveContexts() and
TryForciblyEvictedContexts() to avoid allocating new Oilpan collection
holders during teardown.

Co-authored with Gemini.

Fixed: 583036777
Test: blink_unittests, blink_platform_unittests

892eb9dda40668c51d469f945b08a365b08d1375
parent
aeb7af33de2d27d0def8d689dc779c19f9ebac8f [ diff ] 
[CrOS] Defer SmbService host locator and network observer registration

SmbService::CompleteSetup() currently registers mDNS and NetBIOS host
locators and a NetworkChangeObserver unconditionally for every user
session. As a result, every network change runs mDNS and NetBIOS LAN
discovery (opening UDP sockets, punching firewall holes, and waking
smbproviderd if any LAN host replies) even when the user has never
configured or searched for SMB shares.

Defer registering the host locators and NetworkChangeObserver until
saved or preconfigured shares exist in RestoreMounts() or share
discovery is requested via GatherSharesInNetwork().

Bug: b:575247202
Test: unit_tests --gtest_filter=*Smb*
Test: tast run <dut> filemanager.SMB*

parent
4ff36c6b2bbab3a238976cf71cba77c67ed96091 [ diff ] 
[ChroMenu] Show domain for recent tab search results

Previously, recent tab search results displayed their enclosing submenu
title (e.g. "History", "3 tabs" or a device name) as secondary text, and
the commands that restore a whole window, group or split were indexed
alongside the tabs.

Add RecentTabsDynamicMenu::GetTabUrl, which returns the URL of the tab
an action restores, so AppMenuSearchController can show the domain as
secondary text and skip non-tab entries in the History and Other device
tabs submenus.

Bug: 565464199

tree
7bd9addcb7cd0fc08d67f881e09931330f248197
parent
1a21856f25957de276f09224e6bf8164958dc04a [ diff ] 
updater: create the IntegrationTestUserInSystem server in SetUp()

IntegrationTestUserInSystem created its ScopedServer in a default
member initializer, using the constructor that calls EnterTestMode().
That ran while the fixture was being constructed: before
IntegrationTest::SetUp() cleaned up, and also for skipped tests, whose
TearDown() returns early and never calls ExitTestMode(), leaving the
test mode override behind.

Create the server in SetUp(), like the other fixtures do. SetUp()
already enters test mode for both the system and user updaters, so use
the ScopedServer constructor that does not.

Bug: None

Migrate icu::Locale::getDefault to GetDefaultIcuLocale in blink

Migrates icu::Locale::getDefault() to
base::i18n::GetDefaultIcuLocale(), which is the source of truth for the
default ICU locale.

LayoutLocale::GetSystem() now builds the system locale from the BCP47
tag instead of ICU's legacy locale name, which is equivalent for regular
locales.

The Windows font fallback now uses base::i18n::IsTraditionalChinese()
to pick the Traditional Chinese ExtB font. This matches all Traditional
Chinese locales (e.g. zh-HK and zh-MO) instead of only an exact zh-TW
default locale.

This prepares for banning icu::Locale::getDefault() outside of
base/i18n in https://crrev.com/c/8786620.

Migrate icu::Locale::getDefault to GetDefaultIcuLocale in ash

Migrates icu::Locale::getDefault() to
base::i18n::GetDefaultIcuLocale(), which is the source of truth for the
default ICU locale. Where an ICU API requires an icu::Locale, the
LanguageTag is converted with base::i18n::IcuLocaleConverter.

The Projector caption language is now computed as a LanguageTag, using
base::i18n::IsChinese(), and is still stored in the metadata in the
legacy ICU format (e.g. "zh_TW").

ash::GetLanguageTag() now returns the default ICU locale as a
base::i18n::LanguageTag. Its callers, including the Google Photos
wallpaper fetcher in //chrome/browser/ash, set the Accept-Language
header from its tag string, which is never empty. Its test sets the
locale with ScopedDefaultIcuLocale instead of calling
icu::Locale::setDefault(), and the ill-formed locale case is dropped
since such a locale can no longer be the default.

This prepares for banning icu::Locale::getDefault() outside of
base/i18n in https://crrev.com/c/8786620.

media/gpu/windows: Fix D3D12 H.264/H.265 software BRC for screen content

Cast tab mirroring of mostly static pages (for example Google Docs) on
Windows uses D3D12VideoEncodeAccelerator with the software
H264RateController. The output stays blurry at 1.5 to 2.2 Mbps even when
the bitrate cap is 50 Mbps. Three problems in the H.264 and H.265
delegates cause this:

1. The HRD buffer was fixed at 40000 bytes, which is 32 ms at 10 Mbps.
   One 1080p key frame or scroll frame overflows it, and the rate
   controller then pushes the following frames to max QP. The buffer now
   holds one second of the target bitrate, with the old value as a
   floor. It is also updated when the bitrate changes.

2. The rate controller saw kDisplay content but still allowed QP up to
   51 for H.264, which turns screen text into blocks. For kDisplay the
   max QP is now kH264DesktopSWBRCMaxQuantizer (35), the same cap the
   Media Foundation encoder uses for desktop content. H.265 already caps
   at 42.

3. The rate controller timestamp advanced by 1/framerate per frame
   instead of following the frame timestamps. Static pages are captured
   at about 1 fps, so the rate controller saw 30 times the real bitrate
   and raised QP. The frame timestamp is now passed to the delegate and
   mapped to a timeline that starts at zero and never goes backwards.
   Repeated or slightly older timestamps move one frame forward, a
   backward jump of more than one second rebases the timeline, and
   extreme inputs and forward jumps are bounded so the timeline stays
   finite.

Also leave gop_max_duration unbounded (matching Media Foundation and
VAAPI) so long low-fps GOPs do not narrow the size-correction window
mid-GOP, and update HRD buffer parameters in place on frame rate changes
instead of recreating the rate controller.

The H.264 and H.265 delegates had identical software BRC code. It now
lives in a new D3D12SoftwareRateController class that both use.

[🌷] Deflake TrustedWebActivityLocationDelegationTest on Automotive

TrustedWebActivityLocationDelegationTest#verifyLocationFromChrome mocks
the device LocationProvider via LocationProviderOverrider, but omits
enabling the Android system location setting via
LocationSettingsTestUtil.setSystemLocationSettingEnabled(true). On
Automotive emulators where system location is disabled,
GeolocationPermissionContextAndroid denies the request and times out.

Enable the mock system location setting in setUp and
re-enable the test on Automotive. Register ResettersForTesting in
LocationProviderFactory#setLocationProviderImpl and add
LocationUtils#setFactoryForTesting (used by LocationSettingsTestUtil)
so static location overrides reset automatically after each test
without manual @After tearDown().

aeb9136b4f937ac05b67fbd219c555ad4a19992b [ diff ] 
Run taskbar pin flows on a COM STA thread

Pin flows currently run on a plain SequencedTaskRunner in the
multi-threaded apartment (MTA). While WinRT's ITaskbarManager is
ThreadingModel=Both, shell COM objects used on the pin sequence (such as
CLSID_TaskbandPin / IPinnedList3, which is registered as
ThreadingModel=Apartment with no cross-apartment proxy/stub, and
IShellLink) require a single-threaded apartment (STA).

Switch the pin sequence to a shared COM STA task runner so both
ITaskbarManager and the Windows 10 IPinnedList3 fallback run in a COM
STA.

8cd36fc55033872194361caf8e7feec8647edffc [ diff ] 
Lazy copy desktop capture frames

DesktopCapturereAndroid copy every frame from VirtualDisplay even though
DesktopCaptureDevice only consumes frames at the lower capture rate
(e.g. 60-120Hz vs 30/15 fps). Most copies are unused.

Now only the latest frame is held and copied in CaptureFrame(). Unused
frames will be released without copying. This change also
- Move the bounds CHECKs into CheckRgbPlane()
- Raise ImageReader maxImages to 3
- Add kDesktopCaptureAndroidLazyFrameCopy switch

On a 60Hz device, capture thread CPU dropped ~14% at 30 fps and 33% at
15 fps.

BUG=581682340

gpu: Enable texture storage for float and half-float RG formats

When initializing feature validators with GL_EXT_texture_rg, FeatureInfo
previously omitted GL_R32F_EXT, GL_RG32F_EXT, GL_R16F_EXT, and
GL_RG16F_EXT from texture_internal_format_storage even when the
corresponding float and half-float pixel types were supported. In
addition, GL_RED_EXT was erroneously added to texture_internal_format
during ext_texture_norm16 setup.

Because SharedContextState initializes FeatureInfo with an ES2 context
type without calling EnableES3Validators(), GLCommonImageBackingFactory
computed supports_storage as false for SinglePlaneFormat::kR_F16 (as
well as kRGBA_F16 and kR_F16 on ES3 contexts without
GL_OES_texture_half_float) and fell back to glTexImage2D instead of
glTexStorage2DEXT even when ext_texture_storage was enabled. This also
prevented TexImageTexStorageEquivalence tests from asserting that
TexImage2D and TexStorage2D support are equivalent across all
uncompressed single-plane formats.

This CL:
- Adds GL_R32F_EXT/GL_RG32F_EXT and GL_R16F_EXT/GL_RG16F_EXT to
  texture_internal_format_storage in FeatureInfo::InitializeFeatures()
  when ext_texture_rg and the corresponding pixel types are supported,
  and removes the extraneous GL_RED_EXT from ext_texture_norm16.
- Overrides GL_RED_EXT to GL_R16F in
  GLFormatCaps::ToGLFormatDescOverrideHalfFloatType() when GL_HALF_FLOAT
  is used on ES3 without GL_OES_texture_half_float.
- Updates GLCommonImageBackingFactory to check ES3 half-float support
  for GL_R16F and GL_RGBA16F when determining storage support.
- Tightens TexImageTexStorageEquivalence tests to assert
  supports_tex_image == supports_tex_storage.

Bug: None

Don't drop nested worker termination notices during parent shutdown
📋 Metadata
commit
8bbbf3e910bc7a85e86b1be21a4f9a230495cef0
[ log ]
author

tree
6eebe0d3d1d3a3681978e85afed157629704916e
parent
c1823d0bceb50cb9aa272d199f367e6670717fc4 [ diff ] 
Don't drop nested worker termination notices during parent shutdown

A parent worker can't finish shutting down until each nested worker has
reported that it terminated. That report is posted to the parent's
internal task queue, which the parent deletes early in its own shutdown.
If the report is still queued when the queue is deleted, the task is
lost, leaking both OS worker threads until the renderer exits.

This change fixes the leak by wrapping the termination notice in a small
fallback helper. While the parent is alive, the notice runs on the
normal queue in order and respects pausing; if that queue is deleted
before the notice runs, the wrapper's destructor reposts it to the
parent thread's cleanup queue. This scheme follows the same pattern
Blink's scheduler uses for non-nestable deletions. Because a parent
worker keeps its backing thread and cleanup queue alive until every
child's notice has run, a rescued notice is always executed.

For testing this change, we extend DedicatedWorkerTest helpers to
support starting nested workers from a worker global scope (copying
WorkerSettings from the parent worker and allowing nested RunLoops
during startup) and observing child termination handshakes.

>   and verify that the CoreML backend is used on macOS 14.4+ ARM64.

1a0f095f661cf386e17ecd0cad587ad14d14a404
parent
732f5295d86f5efbe1ce4dfacdea84965c8ca646 [ diff ] 
[mp-prefs] Add a key-scoped observer API to SharedPreferencesManager

Callers that want to know when a preference changes currently register
an OnSharedPreferenceChangeListener directly on
ContextUtils.getAppSharedPreferences(). That attaches the listener to a
single file, requiring callers to know which file their key lives in
once managers can be bound to named files.

Adds SharedPreferenceObserver (implementing Destroyable) and @CheckResult
SharedPreferencesManager#observe(...) overloads for single-key
(Runnable) and multi-key (Callback<String>) subscriptions. Each
subscription checks its keys against the manager's PreferenceKeyChecker,
binds to the manager's backing SharedPreferences at creation time, and
unregisters on destroy(), keeping SharedPreferencesManager stateless.

Design: go/clank-mp-sharedpreferences §5.6
Behaviour change for existing callers: none. No listener is registered
until observe() is called, and nothing calls it yet; existing direct
listeners keep working unchanged.

Tests: base_junit_tests (SharedPreferencesManagerTest: single-key and
multi-key delivery, destroy, no cross-file notification, cross-manager
notification on the same file, null-key ignore, and registry checks).

Support running traffic annotation auditor in Cog workspaces

Enable auditor.py to run in Cog and non-git worktrees by querying
untracked, non-ignored files using a temporary bare git repository.
Exclusion rules are populated from .gitmodules, .gclient_entries, and
safe_list.txt to ignore submodules, third-party checkouts, and
safe-listed directories.

Additionally, optimize GN timestamp checks using build.ninja.d when
present, use ThreadPoolExecutor on Cog to optimize for network/RPC file
access, and update relevant skill documentation and tests.

📋 Metadata

53253455a5f498d6bd2cc86e881f25b28eb04830

tree
664aaa25219b5c4232bf126c4ecba7d3ddb98416
parent
c0b1b2016a0e107b5e8097598d9bfdd8891ac282 [ diff ] 
checkdeps: Require specific_include_rules keys to match whole filename

checkdeps used re.match to match specific_include_rules keys against a
file's basename, so a key only needed to match a prefix of the name.
This made several broken keys appear to work by accident, e.g.
"foo*" (regex: "fo" followed by any number of "o"s), "foo\\." or
"foo\\.[cc|h]" (a character class matching the "c" of ".cc").

Switch to re.fullmatch and fix all keys in the tree that relied on the
prefix semantics:
- "foo*" -> "foo.*"
- "foo\\." -> "foo\\.(cc|h)"
- "foo\\.[cc|h]" -> "foo\\.(cc|h)"
- ".*test\\.c" -> ".*test\\.cc"
- ".*_unittest\\.(cc|h|m)" -> ".*_unittest\\.(cc|h|mm)"
- "server_environment" -> "server_environment\\.(cc|h)"
- "(test_configurator|.*_unittest\\.(cc|h))" ->
  "(test_configurator|.*_unittest)\\.(cc|h)"
- ".*test.(cc|h)" -> ".*test\\.(cc|h)" in two places where it also
  matched "*_test_helper(s).{cc,h}" by accident (none of these rely on
  the rule).

No key in the tree now matches a file under re.match but not under
re.fullmatch, and checkdeps passes.

[Optimization Guide Tests] Expand Linux CPU AI WPT pool capacity

Remove the gce mixin from the four Linux CPU AI WPT suites on
optimization_guide-linux. Restricting those 32 shards per build to
gce:1 funnels every trybuild onto only 16 Ubuntu GCE VMs in
chrome.tests.intelligence, causing shards and gtests to expire in the
queue while 73 Ubuntu non-GCE hosts sit available.

Removing gce allows Linux CPU AI WPT shards to run on any Ubuntu x86-64
bot in the pool, matching win_cpu_ai_wpt_dimensions on Windows.

📝 Commit Message (First Line)
[WebNN] Remove QueueableResourceState and ResourceTask from LiteRT
📋 Metadata
commit
826483d105fbcef1661f7db7c5fe6dfe551e73be
[ log ]
author
Lynne Jiang <lyjiang@google.com>
Wed Nov 11 03:07:36 2026
committer
Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Wed Nov 11 03:07:36 2026
tree
1402aef0a713f4152bc876b3e5e00d9ef4c6d83c
parent
e94f20022a7c0a00fe2a7260c1609d261e46d2cb [ diff ] 
[WebNN] Remove QueueableResourceState and ResourceTask from LiteRT

Previously, TensorImplTflite and GraphImplLiteRt wrapped their buffer
and compute state in QueueableResourceState and scheduled operations
through ResourceTask queues. Because QueueableResourceState::Unlock()
unconditionally reset the resource state to kUnlocked when any single
shared reader finished, concurrent in-renderer readTensor() calls
allowed a queued writeTensor() or dispatch() to run and free the
underlying BufferContent while another readTensor() or worker dispatch
was still reading from it.

Since WebNNContextImpl already executes on a dedicated per-context
SingleThreadTaskRunner (owning_task_runner_), replace
QueueableResourceState and ResourceTask in the TFLite/LiteRT backend
with direct ownership on the context sequence, matching the CoreML and
ORT backends:
- Store std::unique_ptr<BufferContent> directly on TensorImplTflite,
  guarded by sequence_checker_.
- Store std::unique_ptr<ComputeResources> directly on GraphImplLiteRt
  and run DoDispatch() synchronously on owning_task_runner_.
- In TfliteInRendererTensorHandle, post WriteOnOwningSequence and
  ReadOnOwningSequence directly to owning_task_runner_ in FIFO order,
  copying the read buffer on the owning sequence before invoking the
  renderer callback.

Bug: b:580519360
Test: services_unittests --gtest_filter="*WebNN*"

45e26bcf964f7539bb2d279b135056b15f1615a7 [ diff ] 
OriginVerifier: Own verifier via JniUniquePtr

Currently, OriginVerifier passes raw pointers across the JNI boundary as
long values, which bypasses type safety and lifecycle checks.

Migrate mNativeOriginVerifier to JniUniquePtr<NativeOriginVerifier>. C++
constructs OriginVerifier via jni_zero::MakeUnique and passes ownership
to Java, which destroys the instance via cleanUp() and
JniUniquePtr.destroy().

tree
a2dd0b467eadf1de2b121b64111d7b014726e912
parent
c5cf1b3837a53460da759a2339e235e65ed254c3 [ diff ] 
[base] Add event-based Windows MessagePumpForIO

Windows Basic Processes restrict system calls and require an alternative
to IOCP for asynchronous I/O. Add an event-backed pump while preserving
the registration and ownership contracts of existing IOCP consumers.

Share the run loop and idle-wait logic in MessagePumpForIO, with
backend-specific completion waits and task wakeups. The event backend
uses explicit completion-event watches whose scoped controllers own the
events and watch registrations, rather than emulating IOCP registration.

Select the backend during process startup through a disabled-by-default
renderer feature. The feature applies to existing Win32 renderers
independently of Basic Process enablement, allowing correctness and
performance A/B testing before sandbox integration. Other process types
continue to use IOCP.

Adapt ChannelWin to use read/write completion events when supported,
preserving the existing cancellation policy and asynchronous shutdown
semantics. This prepares renderer I/O for Basic Processes without
enabling the sandbox or redesigning other I/O consumers.

Add tests for factory selection, event watches and task wakeups,
cross-backend channel communication, and shutdown with both backends.

1abb797a938e3de4487edd9fdea4e8bc50a847b5
parent
3986ec190fa00815d7359909a993d2372f0b6b12 [ diff ] 
Build the Linux snap package directly with mksquashfs

//chrome/installer/linux/snap/build.py previously invoked `snapcraft
pack`, which required snapd/LXD on the build host and fetched unpinned
Ubuntu 26.04 stage-packages at build time.

Assemble the snap tree directly in build.py and pack it with mksquashfs:
- Replace snapcraft.yaml.in with snap.yaml.in, expanding the plugs,
  environment, hooks, layout, and command-chain entries from the
  core26 gnome extension so meta/snap.yaml is generated directly.
- Merge the pinned Ubuntu 26.04 runtime bundle (prime/ and copyright/)
  from //chrome/installer/linux/snap/runtime/linux-<arch>, unwrapping
  CIPD symlink-mode checkouts without dereferencing internal symlinks.
- Generate meta/gui/<snap>.desktop, meta/hooks/configure, clean
  command-chain trampolines into the gnome-core26 and mesa-2604
  content snaps, content-snap mount directories, the xdg-email ->
  /usr/bin/xdg-open symlink, and a plain-text help page + bin/man
  wrapper so --help works without bundling GPL man-db/groff binaries.
- Exclude libqt5_shim.so, since the runtime bundle only contains Qt 6
  libraries, and clear the setuid bit on chrome-sandbox.
- Normalize staging timestamps to build_timestamp and invoke mksquashfs
  with the flags `snap pack` uses (-comp lzo -no-fragments -all-root
  -no-xattrs -mkfs-time -root-time).
- Assert in BUILD.gn when enable_snap_package=true that the runtime
  checkout is present, with instructions to set checkout_snap_runtime in
  .gclient custom_vars.

enable_snap_package remains false until the 3pp builder uploads the
initial runtime CIPD package and //DEPS adds checkout_snap_runtime.

[ActorSurface] Make ActorSurface own ActorTabData

11176d35b5ec0797899670034c26eca71624b455

author

Wed Nov 11 02:52:37 2026
tree
5c88c4e72c44cdb243f6fa411202147ec2bba4a0
parent
9e5217f400d567685ab294470b8764f823b6424f [ diff ] 
[ActorSurface] Make ActorSurface own ActorTabData

ActorTabData was a TabFeature for tabs and owned by ActorSurfaceImpl
only for headless surfaces, so it was reset whenever a surface was
promoted to a tab or demoted back to headless. Make every
ActorSurfaceImpl own its ActorTabData for its whole lifetime, and stop
creating it in TabFeatures.

- ActorSurface::GetActorTabData() is never null now; drop the dead null
  checks on it.
- Remove ActorTabData's tab constructor, user data and
  From(TabInterface*). Tab-based callers look the data up through the
  tab's ActorSurfaceHandle.

[PiP] Remove fullscreen-video-picture-in-picture from chrome://flags

The fullscreen-video-picture-in-picture entry reaches its expiry
milestone in M160. The underlying kFullscreenVideoPictureInPicture
feature is enabled by default and stays, because desktop Android turns
it off through a feature override in chrome_browser_field_trials.cc.

Remove only the chrome://flags entry, its descriptions and metadata.

[WebNN] CoreML: Reject pad operations with dimensions exceeding 16384
📋 Metadata

73545ee23abd670dbb0fde5f8c57390380d952bc
[ log ]
author
Phillis Tang <phillis@chromium.org>
Wed Nov 11 02:42:27 2026
committer
Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Wed Nov 11 02:42:27 2026
tree
3c5d33bfbb055bc4812a732779d792bdd8941e66
parent
7b8fa1f2dc16dbd60cb6b3afcbe208918c703e91 [ diff ] 
[WebNN] CoreML: Reject pad operations with dimensions exceeding 16384

When a pad operation has a dimension exceeding 16384 and CoreML falls
back to the legacy Espresso engine, Espresso rejects its native MPS pad
layer and delegates to MPSNDArrayPad (padArrayData), which dispatches
uniform (32, 8, 1) threadgroups without bounds-checking the outer
dimension and writes out of bounds on the GPU when the product of the
outer dimensions is not a multiple of 8.

Return NewNotSupportedError when any pad output dimension exceeds 16384,
as normal models do not need to exceed this limit so emulation is
unnecessary.

[Actor] Remove the duplicate margin setter on the overlay view

The overlay view had its own margin setter that copied the binder's
margin helper line for line, and the binder was its only caller. The
binder now sets the overlay view's top margin through the same helper
it uses for the container, and the view's copy is removed.

[X11] Do not classify absolute pointer devices as styluses

DeviceDataManagerX11::UpdateDeviceList previously treated any slave or
floating pointer device with "Abs X" or "Abs Y" valuators
(DT_STYLUS_POSITION_X / DT_STYLUS_POSITION_Y) as a stylus. Because all
absolute pointing devices expose "Abs X" and "Abs Y" valuators, mice
using absolute positioning (such as HP Anyware PCoIP remote desktop
virtual pointers, QEMU/VirtualBox/SPICE VM tablets, and XWayland
pointers) were misclassified as EventPointerType::kPen, reporting
pointerType="pen" and pointerId>=2 instead of pointerType="mouse" and
pointerId=1.

Require stylus-specific valuators ("Abs Pressure", "Abs Tilt X", or
"Abs Tilt Y") or an explicit XInput 1 stylus/eraser/tablet device type
atom ("STYLUS", "ERASER", "TABLET") before classifying a device as a
stylus, and exclude non-stylus devices that may also expose an
"Abs Pressure" valuator (touchpads, touchscreens, and tablet pucks or
button pads).

Fixed: 489447085
